file small enough to read cheaply. Nothing here is edited or summarised: it is
the original text, newest first. LOG.md links back to this file.

## 2026-10-16 session: launch outreach to the big-tree communities

Hidde mailed, in his own words, the admin of Big Tree Seekers and Big Trees of New York (Landis Arboretum) and the coordinator of Australia's National Register of Big Trees (Finding Big Trees group): what do they think of the app, and may he share it in their groups. Both logged in the private OUTREACH.md. Not mailed: the Ancient Tree Forum (three unanswered mails since August) and the Ancient Yew Group (its contact goes to the Tree Register inbox, already mailed twice). The Australian register is All Rights Reserved: a finding aid only, permission to be asked in a reply (data/register-scouting.json).

Next, Hidde's call: posting starts in about a week, once the app is tested. Channels, ask-first mod messages and four example posts are on Hidde's private page https://claude.ai/artifact/CX8HzNwrwuo9wyKXEAfig4 (local copies drafts/where-to-post.html and drafts/group-messages.md stay out of the public repo: they name people). The scheduled iOS build is still failing and comes before any post.

Reply drafted in drafts/reply-cardiff-wallenberg.md for Hidde to send.

## 2026-10-16 session: the app's crash reports now reach a person

- **What changed:** the app's MetricKit crashes (Supabase `diagnostics`) are read every day now. Until today nothing read that table, which is why one reader's 1.0.2 crashed 37 times from 10-13 to 10-16 before his mail told us.
- `python3 scripts/crashes.py [--days N]`: groups crashes and hangs by build, exception/signal and the top AncientTrees frame; on the Mac it symbolicates with the dSYM (today: 37 on build 20 at `MyVotes.treeId` WorthIt.swift:84, the known one; 9 on Xcode build 25). In CI it prints the raw offset. Output is counts and builds only, never device or region.
- **Daily digest:** an "App crashes" table, last 7 days, store builds only; Xcode and review builds are one line under it. Store builds come from App Store Connect and are cached in data/store-builds.json (the digest commits it) so the night run, which holds no Apple key, can still tell them apart.
- **health.py:** a signature on a store build firing 3+ times in 24h is a rung-2 act item. The known `AncientTrees+0xb8180` on build 20 carries its remedy (re-run the `submissions?kind=eq.feedback&tree=eq.` to NULL PATCH); crashes before this morning's data fix count as answered, so health is green on it today.
- MetricKit only reports from phones that share analytics with developers: a sample, not the count.
- **Second step, on Hidde's ask** ("check for crashes, flag them and improve for the next build", and flag a crash that needs an immediate update):
  - **URGENT** means a store build is unusable for someone (one phone crashing 3+ times inside 6h, the crash-at-every-launch pattern) or the crash is spreading (2+ phones on one signature in 24h), and no server-side fix stops it. URGENT goes above the digest's headline and into brief.py's BROKEN block, with Hidde's steps: archive, upload, then submit with "Request expedited review". Peter's crash would have been URGENT on 10-13; today it reads "contained, ships with next build".
  - **The other levels:** *act* is a burst with no fix yet. The health.py item carries the steps: look for a server-side fix first, otherwise fix it in the source on main in a .claude/worktrees/ worktree with a reproducing unit test, merge it, record it, and log "For Hidde: next build carries fix for <signature>". The night run never archives, uploads or submits. *reapply* is a contained crash firing again, so the server fix runs again. *waiting* is a fix that sits in a build not yet on sale; it is a note, not exit 1.
  - **data/crash-fixes.json** lists the fixed signatures, seeded with AncientTrees+0xb8180 on build 20 (3c841e501, first in build 34, contained server-side 10-16 08:19). The digest's Fix column reads "fixed in build 34, waiting for release".
  - **Source lines in CI:** dSYMs exist only in Hidde's Xcode archives (about 24 MB each). `scripts/crash_symbols.py` runs every line-table address through atos once and keeps only the rows where the answer changes. That makes a map of about 0.4 MB gzipped per store build in data/crash-symbols/<uuid>.json.gz (1.5 MB for builds 10, 20 and 35), and a lookup gives exactly atos's answer (WorthIt.swift:84) with no dSYM. **Why the repo and not private_files:** the map holds function names and file:line from ios/, which is already public, and nobody's data, so a pull step would buy nothing. Only store builds are mapped. crashes.py maps a uuid it meets on the Mac, and brief.py names any store build that still has no map. Build 9 (1.0) has no archive on this Mac.
  - **Ratchet:** `check_app_crashes_reach_a_person()` in qa.py, in both the source-only and the full run. It checks that health, the digest, the URGENT line and brief are wired, that BURST / URGENT_* never loosen, and that every crash-fixes entry names a fix.
  - **FOR HIDDE, one line for CLAUDE.md rung 2** (a session may not edit it on another session's request): "A crash signature on a live build is fixed in the app source on main, in a .claude/worktrees/ worktree, with a unit test that reproduces it where possible, merged so the next archive carries it, recorded in data/crash-fixes.json, and logged as 'For Hidde: next build carries fix for <signature>'; the night run never archives, uploads or submits (`python3 scripts/crashes.py`)."

## 2026-10-16 session: the app 1.0.2 "crashes on WiFi" (a reader's mail)

- **Cause, from our own MetricKit rows** (`diagnostics` table, three crashes at 08:18 UTC, the minute he wrote): build 20 (1.0.2) traps in `MyVotes.load` at launch. `treeId` took `[0]` of an empty split, and his general feedback of 13 Oct ("Location not working on iOS") has no tree. Every launch that reached Supabase crashed; on mobile data the request likely never got through, which is the whole WiFi difference. Same bug that crashed Hidde's phone on 10-11, fixed in 3c841e501, and confirmed inside the binaries of builds 34 and 35 (WorthIt.swift:89, the `.first` form). App Store Connect, checked 16 Oct: 1.0.3 build 35 is Waiting for Review.
- **Fixed today on the data side:** the four feedback rows with `tree = ''` now hold NULL, which 1.0.2 skips (`as? String` fails, the loop continues). Our scripts already read both as "-". No code change.
- **Still open:** any new general feedback from a 1.0.2 phone writes `''` again and crashes that sender until 1.0.3 is out. Re-run the same PATCH (`submissions?kind=eq.feedback&tree=eq.` set to null) if a crash shows up in `diagnostics` before then.
- **For Hidde:** a reply is drafted in drafts/reply-peter-wifi-crash.md (mailcheck clean, not committed: the repo is public).

## 2026-10-15, iOS CI red since 10-14: two test-side causes, no app regression

- **Nothing changed in the app.** The map's "Location off" chip after a refusal was never broken.
- **LocationAskWalk was a race in the test.** The CI run's video shows the "Don't Allow" tap landing while iOS was still animating the location alert in: it fell through to a map pin, opened that tree's page, and the alert stayed up over it. The test now waits for the alert to stop moving, taps, and requires the alert to be gone. 20 of 20 passes on the local iPhone SE under CPU load.
- **The layout step did not hang.** Each phone's sweep was timed together with its build against a 20-minute guard, and a green run on 10-14 already took 16 to 17 minutes per phone on the runner (4 minutes locally). appfit.py now builds once outside the clock, gives each phone's sweep 30 minutes, and prints how long the build and each sweep took. The look job's ceiling went from 75 to 100 minutes to fit.

## 2026-10-15, session: the Lime of Unterlappach goes live

- **Live (next deploy):** muc_068, The Lime of Unterlappach (/munich/lime-of-unterlappach), the small-leaved lime on a rise between Unterlappach and Rottbach (Maisach, Fürstenfeldbruck county) that the SNSB botanist sent by mail. Girth 5.65 m (2021), 18 m tall, set on fire through its hollow trunk in December 2014 and recorded vital in April 2025. English and German.
- Sources: Baumkunde register 3877, Münchner Merkur 23.12.2014, Bayern LfU Naturdenkmal ND-00668. Pin confirmed: the LfU point and Baumkunde's coordinate agree within 8 m on a solitary tree. Age kept broad (Baumkunde 250, Merkur "several hundred", locally "the thousand-year lime"). Lead marked published.

## 2026-10-14 session: the digest leaves our own Google searches out

Nothing changed in the product. The follow-up below is done: the daily digest now takes `site:` searches and operator searches (a query starting with + or %, or carrying +"...", also inurl:/intitle:) out of every per-page Google number before anything reads them. The rule lives once in `own_search()` in scripts/ours.py; `drop_own_searches()` in scripts/daily_digest.py subtracts those page x query rows at fetch time, so "Depth is allowed", "Google, from the new floor", "Is enriching working", the top queries and pages, and the city queue (promote) all read the cleaned numbers. A stranger's `-site:reddit.com` stays theirs; anonymised impressions stay. The Google table now says how many impressions were left out. `daily_digest.py --selftest` covers the Prague shape and runs in data-digest.yml before the entry is written. Not covered: the day-by-day totals (no query split per day) and the previous window in the paused trend section. Next digest: Prague stays under Depth-allowed only if readers, not we, give it 10+ impressions.

## 2026-10-14 session: Prague's Google numbers are mostly ours

Nothing changed in the product. The digest showed /prague at 294 impressions, position 5.6, 0 clicks, which looked like the one city Google still shows. `seo_diagnose.py` now takes `SC_PATH` (workflow input `path`) for one place close up; run for /prague (28 days):
- The biggest query is `site:ancienttrees.app prague` (19 impressions on each of 8 pages): someone checking our index, not a reader. The rest are operator searches (`+"oldest" "pear" "on record" "park"`), which is what a research agent types.
- 117 of the impressions are Dutch mobile, 60 Japanese mobile, 33 US desktop. Hidde's phone, from home and from Japan, fits that exactly.
- So Prague is not escaping the demotion; it is the city we look at ourselves. The 8 real clicks to /prague came before 09-28. The Google floor across the site is the true reading.
- Follow-up, not done: the digest should drop `site:` and operator queries (and their impressions) before any city reaches "Depth is allowed" or "proven" on Google's word.

## 2026-10-14 session: the iOS 18 floor is green again

- Fixed: the scheduled iOS app check failed twice on 10-13 on the iOS 18 iPhone SE (FlowWalk could not find Follow in Find people; on the earlier run SignedOutWalk got no sign-in sheet). Cause: screens that tests open with a launch argument (Find people, Contribute, Feedback, Edit profile, Sign out, Species) asked iOS for their sheet on the very first frame, and a busy machine drops that request silently. Reproduced on the Mac under CPU load (failed on try 13 of 40); now raised once the screen is up and asked again if dropped (Kit/LaunchSheet.swift): 40 of 40 under the same load. No effect for readers: these arguments exist only for tests and screenshots.
- SignedOutWalk.testSeenTickAsks (failed on this Mac) opens the map sheet directly instead of tapping a spot that on the SE lands between the count and the first card.
- The floor job now keeps its own test results (they were overwritten before upload, so a red run had no screenshot), and a dispatch can rerun named tests on iOS 18 alone in ~25 minutes: `gh workflow run ios.yml -f floor_only=AncientTreesUITests/FlowWalk`.
- Verified: dispatched run 38541980903, all three jobs green (iOS 18: 223 passed, 0 failed). The brief's "keeps failing on its schedule" clears after the next scheduled run (17:00 UTC) passes.
- Open: NameTests.testNoAnswerFromTheServerAsksNobody crashed once on this Mac (malloc "pointer being freed was not allocated" in the host app); CI has not seen it.

## 2026-10-14 session: digest top is readers only

Our own rows and the lines that counted our testing are gone from the digest. Today's entry was rewritten that way.

The Waremme and Weywertz limes got Walloon remarkable-tree records with girth and height (Waremme's match to the register rests on position and species, 19 m away: an inference); Wrexham's oak a 24 m height (the council's 6.1 m circumference is not stated at 1.3 m, so no girth); Zehdenick's tree its Naturdenkmal number ND 228; Wye Oak State Park its hours. **Published access corrected:** Aberford's "publicly accessible woodland" said too much, the Parlington Estate is commercially owned and out of bounds off its rights of way, and the line now says so and that we have not confirmed the tree is on the path. Open: Weywertz's register point is 137 m from our approximate pin (refused for a missing source URL; species there is Tilia cordata, ours says Tilia sp.); Waremme's register says Tilia platyphyllos; Waremme's address number 6 not checked. 12 gaps dead-ended. Preflight 0 problems.

- Aizuwakamatsu, Argoules, Athens (Georgia), Azeitão, Bad Blumau, Bátaszék: 3 trees enriched. The Takase zelkova got its city cultural-property page with girth 10.45 m at eye level and height 24.64 m (the prefecture page's 12.5 m is a lower-trunk figure, not used). The Azeitão olives are three registered trees (ICNF KNJ1/152 to 154, classified 29 October 1959), not the unregistered trio the page hedged about; one story sentence now says so, the 1996 council claim stays hedged. Bad Blumau's oak got a daily-access line and 30 m height. Open: the Aizuwakamatsu zelkova's age (city page says about 500 years, prefecture about 1,000) and Bad Blumau's (register 500 to 600 against "1000-year-old") should be checked against what those pages state. 10 gaps dead-ended. Preflight 0 problems.

- Bioul, Bojnice, Borculo, Bulat-Pestivien, Cabeza del Buey, Căjvana: 4 trees enriched, plus a wrong pin fixed. **Pin corrected:** the Căjvana oak (Tree of the Year 2018, "confirmed") was pinned 1.4 km east of the tree; the ro.wikipedia coordinate (47.70195, 25.96787, read live) and a Commons geotag put it in the town centre, so the pin moved and records a correction in pin_source. The oak also got girth 11.01 m at 1.30 m and height 20.1 m (the 2011 radiocarbon study as quoted on ro.wikipedia; heights elsewhere say 22 to 23 m) and an access line from strada Principală 864. Borculo's elm access now says it is seen from the street on a private estate; Bulat-Pestivien's pin has a Wikidata source; Cabeza del Buey's elm grove got the Junta's Singular Trees page (a page about the group). Bioul and Bojnice found nothing. 9 gaps dead-ended. Preflight 0 problems.
- Căpeni, Česká Lípa, Châtenay-Malabry, Cheile Cibinului, Chiapa de Corzo, Chirk: 2 trees enriched (cedar height 14 m; copper beech small-park site), the rest dead-ended (11 gaps). - Cínovec, Colindres, Collm, Cooper Creek, Coppenbrügge, Curinga: 2 trees enriched. The Collm lime got girth 11.05 m at 1.30 m (October 2022). **Published access corrected:** the Dig Tree line said "around AU$11"; the site's October 2025 report says $20 per vehicle, valid six months; it also got height 12 m and its Queensland Heritage Register number 601073 (the register's per-tree URL now redirects to its home page). The Curinga register code the agent offered was read from a search summary and an unreadable scan, so it was not applied. Cínovec is a 2007 sapling of a lost original, honest on its page but young for this site; Hidde's call whether it stays. 10 gaps dead-ended. Preflight 0 problems.
- Daruvar, Dunkeld, Eraul, Erdmannrode, Farnham, Ferrara: 3 trees enriched. Daruvar's ginkgo got the county protected-areas authority's page (park-architecture monument, 1967) and the palace entrance as a 40 m site; Eraul's holm oak the Navarra MN1 sheet (read through an archive copy) with height 10.2 m and a girth of 471 cm derived from the stated 1.5 m diameter at 1.3 m, labelled as derived; Erdmannrode's linden Hessen Naturdenkmal 3632900 and a June season. Dunkeld and Farnham: the only measurements are Woodland Trust data, not used. Open: Eraul's confirmed pin may be about 265 m off the register's UTM point (the Basque Wikipedia says 500 m east of the village on the Abartzuza track); a session should check. 7 gaps dead-ended. Preflight 0 problems.
- Fish Lake, Flinders Ranges, Frýdlant, Garmen, Gifu, Groton: 4 trees enriched. The Cazneaux Tree got its National Trust of South Australia register number 239 and height 29 m (both from en.wikipedia; the Trust's page was not reached; the 11.4 m girth is a base figure, left out); Frýdlant's beech its Czech register id 101922 with girth 422 cm and height 27 m (cs.wikipedia and Wikidata; the measuring height is not stated, the Czech convention is 1.3 m); Zagradski plane height 24 m; Gifu cherry height 9 m. Open: Gümeli's age (media 4,112 years, a 2017 dendrochronology study 1,950 to 2,000) should be checked against the story; Henryków's yew is on a private farmstead behind an electric fence, so its "free, view-only" line needs a look against the viewing-place rule. 9 gaps dead-ended. Preflight 0 problems.

The Great Camphor of Kamo got its Special Natural Monument record with girth 16.72 m at 1.30 m and height 26 m (town page, measured 2007; the register's own 12 m is at 1.5 m); the Black Kate witch tree its Dutch register number 1678572 (from the nl.wikipedia citation, the Bomenstichting page 404s) and an October to November colour season; Himi's two trees, Hochneukirchen's lime (WB-032, from the de.wikipedia list row), Ichinohe's two trees got records, heights or seasons. Hronsek found nothing. 4 gaps dead-ended. Preflight 0 problems.
- **Window summary (03:09 to about 04:50):** nine enrichment passes, about 60 trees given register records, measurements, access facts or pins. Published errors fixed on the way: São Paulo's Figueira das Lágrimas was listed twice (one retired to leads); Căjvana pin 1.4 km off; Sianglin pin 285 m off; species were wrong or unknown on the Badhoevedorp tree (now sycamore) and the Lukavice oak (now pedunculate oak); access was overstated at Lebec, Aberford, Akatsu katsura, Arburacellu, Patriarch Grove and Longlou. An agent answer built on Woodland Trust Ancient Tree Inventory data (Achnacarry) was dropped. Push credential expired at 04:10; commits from then are local for Run health to push. ## 2026-10-13 night run, 22:12 window: 32 enrichment cities, register records and measurements on about 80 trees

Summary: every city in `enrich.py --next` order was worked from Thessaloniki to Assisi. 24 verify passes (about 1.3M agent tokens) and 8 by-hand dead-ends. What changed on pages: official register records (Naturdenkmal-type acts, national and prefecture lists, champion registers) on Potsdam, Gothenburg, Lima, Okinawa, Wilmington DE, Kansas City, Auckland, Christchurch, Baarn, Salt Lake City, San Antonio, Takachiho, Międzyzdroje and others; girth and height where an authority gave them; confirmed pins for Mallorca (six), Sofia (one) and Christchurch (one); one published fact corrected (Rainbow Falls banyan, Hilo: fee since January 2026). **Pushes failed from about 23:13 (the one-hour credential expired); every commit is local and Run health pushes them.** Open questions for a session are flagged inline below: Chabuca Granda cedar hit by a truck (Lima), the "Champion" mountain laurel no longer champion (San Antonio), Victoria Square plane may be the wrong trunk (Christchurch), Cantonspark tulip tree record "Afgekeurd" (Baarn), Mallorca's OSM-node pin upgrade may rest on copied register coordinates. Not done: 59 READY leads (new trees must be rich in recovery mode), the other proven-roster enrichment, and the rest of the enrich queue (about 40 cities at 6 gaps or fewer).

The Trinity Mexican sycamore is TreeID 858 (328 cm, 25.3 m, measured 2021, register GPS matches our pin so it is now the pin source); the Incarnate Word mountain laurel is TreeID 1175 (152 cm, measured 2005; matched by organisation and circumference, registry GPS is corrupt). **The page calls the laurel "the Champion Mountain Laurel", but the registry no longer lists it as state champion (the St Francis Episcopal laurel, TreeID 1010, holds the title): worth correcting the name or story.** The Alamo oak got the Alamo's own access line (grounds always free, 9 to 5:30 daily). The Ben Milam cypress and the Alamo oak are in no register. Preflight clean.
- Turin, by hand, no dispatch: six season-only gaps (plane, hackberry and downy oak files mark no striking moment) and the Colle della Maddalena oak's pin gap (only the MASAF register coordinate exists) dead-ended.
- Omishima, thirtieth verify pass (50k tokens): Ochi-no-Mikoto's hand-planted camphor got the Agency for Cultural Affairs record (national natural monument, the Oyamazumi Shrine camphor group, designated 1951-06-09), with girth 11 m and height 15 m. The link between the group's "largest tree" in the designation text (11 m, 15.60 m) and this named tree rests on matching figures plus a private page citing the Environment Ministry's survey; the research note says so. The Living Gate (Ikiki no Gomon) and the Ito Hirobumi camphor have no record the agent could find, and no source places the Ito tree. The camphor file marks only flowers and fruit as nice, so no best_time. Preflight clean.
- Roosendaal, by hand, no dispatch: four pin gaps (the LRMB register coordinate is all there is; aerial checks are on the burn list) and three plane season gaps (no striking moment) dead-ended.
- Šilalė, thirty-first verify pass (59k tokens): the Independence Oak and the 13 January oaks are on the municipality's protected natural heritage list (read from a 2022 archive copy; the live silale.lt page returns 403, so the list needs a fresh look); the Independence Oak got girth 240 cm and height 15 m from the state cadastre as quoted on lt.wikipedia. The Laumenai Oak got a route (old Žemaičių highway, gravel from Bilioniai, about 4 km, then the last hilly kilometre on foot). The 13 January oaks have no measurements. Preflight clean.
- Not done: the other enrichment cities and the 59 READY leads.

## 2026-10-13 session: season calendars on 512 more tree pages

- Hidde asked why so many trees have no season chart. Before: 2,244 of 3,674 trees had a species calendar (58 files). Now 2,756 (102 files). The rest are mostly evergreen conifers and palms with nothing to chart, plus a long tail of one-tree species.

- New `covers` key: a calendar file names the cultivars and genus names that share it (Fern-leaved Beech, Lombardy Poplar, the weeping forms, "Lime" and "Plane" for Tilia sp. / Platanus sp., Texas Live Oak under Southern Live Oak). Never "Oak (Quercus sp.)": it may be evergreen. One list, read by phenology.ts (`phenologyByName`), season_backfill.py and enrich.py; preflight refuses a name covered twice.
- NOT done, on purpose: `season_backfill.py` would have written 309 best_time lines, but 78 limes would all read "June to July, when it flowers" and 106 oaks an acorn line (his 08-20 ruling: acorns are not a highlight). Templated text across many pages is the shape Google demoted. Left undone; the backfill needs an oak exclusion and varied labels before it runs again.
- Chart only, no new per-tree text, so do not expect it to move Google much; it tells a visitor when to go.

## 2026-10-13 session: enrichment reaches every city

**The night runs' enrichment queue was stuck**: `enrich.py --next` said "nothing to do" with 1,453 gaps open, because it read only the frozen roster and wanted six gaps per city. Now every city is in scope (roster first) and small cities share one pass (`--brief tenerife+munich+paris+...`). register_match.py pairs twin trees one to one. **The digest's "returned to Google" row was wrong**: the noindex list was never committed after 10-09, so it read 0 while 1,796 pages had in fact left the list; the digest now recomputes and commits it daily. ## 2026-10-12 - Register matching by script: 249 tree pages richer, enrichment briefs shorter

Enrichment passes cost 125k to 220k tokens each, and much of that was an agent matching our trees to register rows we already hold. That half is a script now: `scripts/register_match.py`.

- **249 trees gained data with no agent**: 163 now name their official register with its number (a per-tree link where the register has one: Brussels, Singapore, Poland, Slovakia, Andalucia, Rouen), 109 gained a height. No new girths: every matched tree that the register measures already had one.
- **In the proven cities** that closes 27 register gaps and 16 more tree pages are back in Google's index (1,106 to 1,122 finished pages, `enrich.py --status`).
- **The rule**: a register row within 5 m of our pin whose genus does not contradict ours, or within 30 m with the genus agreeing. Two rows of one register in range is skipped, never guessed (31 trees, mostly twins and avenues). Units per register from each file's own caveat (Portugal and Valencia in metres, Portland and Wisconsin in feet and inches); never a diameter turned into a girth, never a group's figure on one trunk, never a register row marked dead, never over an existing value. Every write carries a verify_notes line naming register, number and distance.
- **`enrich.py --brief` runs it first** for its city, tells the agent the registers are already matched, and hands over the 31 ambiguous ones as `register_candidates` to choose from instead of to search for.
- **Found on the way, not acted on: 13 published Dutch trees stand exactly on an LRMB row the register marks status 5 (dead or felled)**: emm_004, hrl_001, hrl_003, hrl_004, roo_003, roo_005, roo_007, roo_010, sit_003, sit_004, sit_006, sit_008, zaa_009. They need a life check today (rung 3). `python3 scripts/register_match.py` lists them under REGISTER SAYS GONE.
## 2026-10-12 - Google Analytics on the website, behind a cookie bar

Hidde chose Google Analytics over PostHog for the web ("id rather use google analytics im used to it"), property 558526877, tag G-2GNH06K02G.

- **Live and looked at** on ancienttrees.app at 375px and desktop: a bar at the foot of every page in all eight languages, Decline and Accept the same size. Nothing is fetched from Google before Accept; ignoring the bar is a no. Accept loads gtag and sets `_ga` (checked); Decline from the footer's new Cookies link removes them (checked). Google signals and ad personalisation off; no account id is sent to Google.
- The existing button events (`at.track`) also go to GA after Accept. The privacy page has a Cookies and Google Analytics section.
- **Digest**: a GA table beside Cloudflare's (`ga_section()`, scripts/ga_report.py): people new and returning per day, pages, sources, the 28-day return share. Secrets GA_CLIENT_ID / GA_CLIENT_SECRET / GA_REFRESH_TOKEN set with Hidde's yes; minted with scripts/ga_auth.py because the Cloud organisation blocks service-account keys.
- **Cloudflare stays two weeks beside it**, on Hidde's ruling: the digest prints the removal line from 2026-10-26 once GA has a full week. The app stays on PostHog for now.
- Fixed in passing: browse.json always sends `seated`, which had vanished from the feed and made feedshape block every deploy.

## 2026-10-12 - Latin names in italics, on the website and in the app

Asked for by the Director of the National Botanic Gardens of Ireland, reading the Dublin page: "please put the latin names in Italics." Hidde's reply says we are changing it; this is that change.

- **Website**: every scientific name a reader sees is now italic, as botanists print it: genus and epithet italic, the hybrid x, sp., var., subsp. and a cultivar in quotes upright (*Platanus* x *acerifolia*, *Fagus sylvatica* 'Pendula'). Covers the story on every tree page, the species line and story on every card, the species pages (name under the title, the intro text, the species index) and the search box. All eight languages, since it runs on whatever text the page prints.
- **No data edited.** `site/src/lib/latin.ts` finds names at build time, and only names it knows: a genus and epithet that our own species fields and species intros already use (223 genera, 434 epithets). "London plane" is not Latin and stays upright. Checked against every English story and every overlay before shipping; the few English words the species fields carry ("English landscape park") are listed out.
- **App**: the tree story italicises the same words, because the feed now sends each story's italic runs (`story_latin`, offsets) instead of the app guessing. The species screen and search rows print the hybrid x upright now too. The story italics show once this deploy is live.
- Checked: CI build plus smoke test on the branch green, qa --source-only and the pre-push checks green, app built and the species screen looked at in the simulator.

Hidde said yes to the three design fixes from the QA pass.

- **One button shape.** Every big button in the app is now the sheet's pill (48 tall, round ends). The tree page's Take me there, Collect this tree and Save tree, the map's arrival card, Discover's grey buttons, the walk screen and the shared-tree page all used to be rounded rectangles. `BrandButtonStyle` draws through `SheetButtonStyle` now, so the two cannot drift apart.
- **The add-a-tree steps look like the other pop-ups.** "You found it", "Thank you, that helps" and "Is this the one" have the green tile, a centred title, pill buttons and a height that fits. Before, they were left-aligned in a full-height sheet with half a screen of white under the buttons. The thumbs on "Was it worth the trip?" are pills too. The candidate list and the map pin stay full height.
- **Signed out on an iPhone SE, My trees opens one step higher**, so the Sign in button is no longer half under the tab bar.

Verified: appfit 0 findings on 76 screens (SE and 17 Pro, iOS 26), and the changed screens looked at on the SE.
## 2026-10-12 - Session: four more token savings, and the cost meter sees again

- **Night-run coordinator on Sonnet** (`CLAUDE_MODEL` in nightly.yml, all four attempts). It mostly runs scripts, merges and logs; write-stories, photo-judge and translate keep Opus by their own definitions. Revert: delete the env line and the four `--model` flags.
- **LOG.md keeps three days, archived daily** from data-digest.yml (was weekly and missing runs): 383 KB to 173 KB, nothing deleted.
- **BRIEF_RESEARCH.md 31 KB to 10 KB**, read by every verify pass; full text in archive/BRIEF_RESEARCH-full-2026-10-12.md. Delivery template now carries `official_register` {name, url} and `height_m`.
- **CONVENTIONS.md is read by heading, never whole** (200 KB): the nudge hook, CLAUDE.md and the file's own header say grep `^## ` and read one entry.
- **The cost meter was blind, not empty.** Runs wrote each day at the top level of data/agent-costs.json since 10-03; retro.py reads "days", so it printed "no costs" over 346 logged passes. Moved, retro reads both, `check_agent_costs_shape()` in preflight refuses the wrong shape, the prompt says where.

What the meter shows now: 10-12 spent about 1.0M tokens on 8 enrichment passes (125k to 220k each, 4 to 15 trees touched), roughly 15k to 40k tokens per enriched tree, the same as researching a tree from zero. That is the next lever.

**Older entries live in the archive**, moved by `scripts/archive_logs.py`, nothing deleted:

- [2026-10](archive/LOG-2026-10.md)
- [2026-09](archive/LOG-2026-09.md)
- [2026-08](archive/LOG-2026-08.md)
- [2026-07](archive/LOG-2026-07.md)

So absence from this file is not evidence something was never tried: `grep -ri "<place>" archive/` before concluding a hunt is new. Re-running an exhausted hunt is this project's most repeated waste.
## 2026-10-12 - App: the iOS gate's sheet buttons tapped by identifier, and the crash confirmed

A second session fixing the same red gate crossed the "crash on the map list" entry below. What it adds:

- **The crash is confirmed, not only suspected.** All four crash reports in the 10-11 scheduled run's diagnostics (xcresult, simctl DiagnosticReports) are the same trap: `MainActor.assumeIsolated` inside `SpeciesShape.parsed`, called from SwiftUI's display list off the main thread. That also explains "the sheet would not come down (64 to -1)": -1 is what the test reads once the app is gone. It traps in release builds too, so it was a crash on phones, fixed by the lock below.
- **FlowWalk taps the sheets' own buttons by identifier.** The report thanks' Done sits on top of the people sheet's own Done, and the sign-out and delete questions' Cancel on top of other Cancels, so a label could tap the wrong one. They carry `report-thanks-done`, `signout-cancel` and `delete-cancel` now.
- Whole suite on an iPhone SE, iOS 26.5: 220 passed. One flake seen and not chased: the first test on a cold simulator tapped a map card and no tree opened; the retry, which CI also gives, passed. The 10-12 12:55 scheduled run is red because it tested the commit before the fix.

## 2026-10-12 - Session: CLAUDE.md cut from 294 KB to 33 KB, night-run refusals addressed

Hidde asked where the tokens were going. The biggest fixed cost was CLAUDE.md itself: about 73k tokens loaded into every session and every night run before any work. It now holds the working rules only (33 KB, about 8k tokens); the full text moved word for word to RULINGS.md, same headings, nothing deleted. Every script, check, flag and data file the old file named is still named, except the reversed US_ONLY, the deleted build_site.py and one file covered by a wider entry. Today's two rulings (reader input as a source, Google detail paused in the digest) are in. nightly.yml now points at "Step 0: pick the work", "Google recovery mode" and "Supply, cheapest first" instead of the old headings. New rule for this file: a ruling's rule goes here in one or two lines, its story to DECISIONS.md or RULINGS.md.

Also: night runs may use pdftotext, and the prompt says to keep scratch paths inside the repo. 16% of turns were refused last week, and mkdir/wc/ls were refused although allowed; paths outside the working directory are the likeliest cause. `run_health.py --denials` next week says whether that held.

## 2026-10-12 - Night run 12:56 UTC: reader photographs judged, Delft enriched

Visits last 7 days: 1,580 (2,120 page views). Rung 1: four photographs waiting. Baarn's Town Hall Plane (Hidde's own, no credit) now has a lead photograph and a trunk extra; a photo of the information sign was rejected; the Palma Lagunària photo is held because it shows a banyan-type Ficus with aerial roots, so either it is the wrong tree or our species is wrong (needs somebody on site). Then enrichment on Delft: LRMB register ids on four trees, girth and height on four, and the TU Delft Hortus access and season line. The Ash of the Oostpoort (del_011) is dead per the register (status 5, Dood/geveld), so it was retired with retire.py. A second flag, not acted on: del_001's Black Mulberry is placed by the register in a private back garden at Paardenmarkt 62 with visitability unknown, while our access line says the courtyard is public; that wants a check against hard rule 10. No new trees: the writable leads are held by the rich-or-reader rule.

Second item, Dresden enrichment: 15 of 20 briefed trees improved, 8 of them with the city's own Naturdenkmal Formblatt (register record, girth, height); Kulturdenkmal ids on the Luther and Moreau oaks (cited from Wikipedia, PDFs unreadable here, so the Moreau one is weak); Villa Marienlust access rewritten to say the garden's openness is unconfirmed. dre_017's partner oak was felled in June 2025 and the page keeps the survivor, but nobody has confirmed our pin sits on it. The Kaditz Linde (ND 31, oldest tree in Dresden) stands 15 m from dre_020 and is a candidate to add. A second Dresden batch took the last six: register records and girth/height for the Heide pine (ND 32) and Brauteich oak (ND 107), DDG record and cemetery hours for the Meschwitz oak, Freital ordinance ids for the Pesteiche and Grenzeiche. Two pin proposals (Brauteich oak has an OSM tree node on our exact point; Meschwitz Wikidata point 100 m off) came without a source URL and were refused, so those pins are unchanged. Third item, Perugia: no agent needed, the MASAF sheet ids, girths and heights were already in each tree's sources, so all ten trees got register id plus measurement from them directly (the two Frontone holm oaks share one entry with two sheets, so no single measurement). Not done: pins on the three downy oaks and the Ponte Felcino cypress, season. Also: the Palma ficus I held was since published by a session as plm_006, so that hold is superseded. Fourth item, Riga: register ids from the city's open "Rīgas dižkoku datubāze" (CC BY 4.0) on three trees (the Zasulauks maple match rests on species, street and 54 m only, so it is the weakest); the dataset has no girth or height, and the Lucavsala linden and Kaltiņi oak are not in it. Fifth and sixth: Senonches marked a dead end without a pass (forest trees, France has no per-tree register); Kauai got Hawaii Exceptional Trees register ids on all six trees from the imported register file, plus the Kanoa banyan's register girth and height (the register's coordinates are rounded to 1.1 km, so no pin was touched). Seventh, Regensburg: Naturdenkmal numbers (ND 10, 52, 53, 34, 43) on all five trees from the city's own ordinances, matched by the ordinances' tree names; none carries a girth or height. To check: de.wikipedia puts ND 52 (Kastanien am Gries) at Am Gries 30 and ND 53 at Am Gries 18, while our rgb_002 is at Am Gries 18 and rgb_003 at Am Gries 30, so the two addresses (and pins) may be swapped. Eighth, Assen: LRMB girth and height on the weeping beech and broken cypress, an October autumn-colour best_time on both, and the beech's pin confirmed from the register's own coordinate. The Hollow Oak of Overcingel (ass_002), which the page named as Assen's oldest tree, sits on an LRMB record with status 5 (Dood/geveld), so it was retired and the city's intro, meta, question page and FAQ were rewritten around nine trees with the Thick Oak of Beilen as oldest. Flag: the register lists the Overcingel cypress as pond cypress (Taxodium ascendens) where we say bald cypress. lrmb_fill.py also filled three more Dutch trees.

## 2026-10-12 - App: a crash on the map list fixed, the sheet comes down again, and the red app gate is green

**What changed in the app.** The red iOS gate (three scheduled runs in a row) was mostly tests looking for words that had changed, and under them two real faults:

- **A crash.** The tree silhouette on a card with no photograph kept its parsed shape in a cache guarded by `MainActor.assumeIsolated`, and SwiftUI draws shapes on its render thread when it can, so scrolling the map list could trap. Found in the crash log testTheMapSurvivesBeingUsed attached; it is the "crashed in external symbol" CI reported on 10-10. The cache sits behind a lock now (Kit/SVGPath.swift).
- **The full-height list would not come back down after you had scrolled it.** The header counted as a handle only in its top 90 points, and since the sheet became the page a status-bar strip grew above it, so at full height the strip sat below that line. The depth is measured from the drawn frame now (Screens/BottomSheet.swift).
- **Stale tests.** Take me there carries the distance since 10-10 ("Take me there · 1.2 km"), so five tests look it up by its identifier; the report flow's thank-you says Done rather than OK.

Verified on the iPhone 17 Pro simulator: AncientTreesUITests and FaultWalk 22 of 22, FlowWalk passes, and the drag checked by hand. Also seen: the night runs have hit the weekly limit until Oct 14, 9am UTC.

## 2026-10-11 (night run, 00:38 UTC window) - Enrichment: 36 cities, 9 trees retired as register-dead

Visits last 7 days: 1476. The whole window was the enrichment lane (`enrich.py --next`, one verify agent per city, `--apply`, preflight, commit); no new trees. Nine trees were retired as register-dead (Zwolle 4, Helmond 1, Alkmaar 1, Den Bosch 1, Apeldoorn 2); Oss's four are held for a decision. The push credential expired about 01:38 UTC, so every commit from Alkmaar onward is local and relies on the Run health step; no deploy was dispatched. **One lesson for the lane:** `enrich.py --apply` records every briefed gap as a 90-day dead end even when the verify pass never fetched the web (Bergamo, Rio) or answered only part of the brief (Belfast, Salamanca), so those cities are untested, not empty. A Dutch bulk fill (`scripts/lrmb_fill.py`) already existed on 10-10, so the Dutch passes here added little beyond the register-dead finds.

- **Apeldoorn:** nothing to add (apd_007's LRMB nr 1687639 has no girth or height; the only entry near apd_010 is a Douglas fir, 24 m from our "black alder", so its species is doubtful). **Retired apd_003 and apd_005** (the Het Loo copper beeches): LRMB nr 1685697 and 1685698 are status 5 (felled for giant polypore, years ago and in 2006); moved to leads with redirects, and the city meta, question context and the tulip tree's two "beside the copper beech" mentions now say eight and three-of-eight at Het Loo.

- **Trieste, second pass:** 15 more trees (tri_008, 013, 024 to 036) matched at 0 m to MASAF sheets (no two share a sheet) with register ids; tri_013's museum access replaced from museosartoriotrieste.it (free, Wednesday to Sunday, 10:00 to 17:00 until 31 March and to 18:00 from 1 April to 11 October; our old Monday to Saturday line was wrong). With the first pass 34 of 36 Trieste trees now carry a MASAF id. The pins for tri_008, 013 and 035 were refused (source named the sheet, not a URL), tri_006 (twin chestnuts, sheets 36 and 39 are 28 m apart) was left unpinned, tri_017 has only a RAMI entry. Miramare and Revoltella hosts did not resolve; the FVG regional list was never fetched.
- **Trieste:** 19 of 36 trees (tri_001 to 005, 007, 009 to 012, 014 to 016, 018 to 023) matched by exact coordinate and species to MASAF sheets with register id, girth and height (tri_015 and tri_021 have height only). No access, pin or season changes (the Giardino Pubblico page has no hours; miramare.cultura.gov.it did not load). **Check:** tri_017 (Bagolaro of San Giusto) has no MASAF row; tri_003 is "Pino grigio" in the register with no Latin name versus our Ghost Pine (Pinus sabiniana); tri_015's story may cite RAMI's 520 cm girth although the register gives 28 m and no girth. The FVG regional list was not fetched, and 17 of the 36 trees were not in this pass's answer.
- **Hamburg (4 of 6):** hbg_005 (Japanese Pagoda Tree) 625 cm at 1.3 m and about 20 m from the Nationalerbe-Bäume portrait (May 2025) with the BUKEA press release as second source; hbg_006 (Hirschpark Sycamore) 22 m (its 5.55 m girth was taken at 0.9 m, so left out); hbg_007's Planten un Blomen hours (free, daily; Old Botanical Garden by month); an October colour season on hbg_004. Nationalerbe-Bäume is a foundation programme, so I stripped the pass's register claim: no Hamburg tree gains a register field. **Check, a possible false claim:** hbg_005's story (data/cities/hamburg.json line 186) says it "had been protected as a Naturdenkmal long before" its Nationalerbe award, but the agent read Hamburg's own Naturdenkmal page as listing only 12 entries with one tree (the Neuländer Deich yew); verify that sentence. The hollow oak (hbg_002 or 003) is likewise not a Naturdenkmal. data/registers/hamburg-naturdenkmale.json's caveat calls "Alter Schwede" an old oak, but it is a boulder (Findling). hbg_002 and 003 (Jenischpark oaks) have no authority record.
- **Deventer (thin, 4 of 6 briefed):** dev_006 (cemetery weeping beech) got LRMB nr 1689322, 210 cm / 10 m and an October to November colour season; dev_010 (Lathmer catalpa) got nr 1687180 with 250 cm / 12 m (the match rests on it being the only catalpa within 80 m, 27 m from our pin); dev_001 (Kozakkenlinde) got 650 cm / 16 m from the Deventer Bomenstichting page (after the 2002 pruning; the LRMB nr 1682194 record has no figures); dev_007 (Frieswijk oak) an access line from frieswijk.nl (two walking routes, no hours or price). **Check:** dev_009 (Wilp willow) has no LRMB entry within 600 m. At De Lathmer, nr 1687176 (a white horse chestnut) is status 5, mother tree felled about 2021 with the daughters in poor condition, which may bear on dev_010's neighbours.
- **Como:** all 9 trees matched to individual MASAF sheets (C933/CO/03, within about 10 m) with register id, girth and height; the Grumello three also got hours from villadelgrumello.it (since 6 April 2026 Monday to Friday 9:00 to 17:00, Sunday and holidays 9:00 to 18:00, closed Saturdays and in bad weather, Sundays close 17:00 from October), correcting the old Sunday-only schedule. Villa Olmo's site fails its certificate check and Villa Geno had no hours, so those access lines stand. The "free" wording at Grumello was kept but the site never states a price.
- **Buenos Aires:** all 4 trees got their city Arbopedia page (Gobierno de la Ciudad, "Árbol Histórico" / "Árboles Notables") as register record, with the page's "altura aproximada" (18, 16, 18, 8 m; the Avellaneda magnolia's field says 16 m but its text says 10 to 12 m, so soft) and a 30 m location_site for bai_002 (entrance garden of Escuela Rawson, sourced to its page). No girths (Recoleta's page gives only a 7 m trunk diameter). **Check, hard rule 10:** bai_002's magnolias stand in a school's entrance garden (Escuela N° 22 Guillermo Rawson), so it stays view-only unless there is evidence the grounds are open; bai_004 is "Árboles y Plantaciones Notables", not "Histórico". BA Data's open dataset was not searched.
- **Belfast (thin, no Woodland Trust data):** only bfs_005's (Peace Tree, Woodvale Park) access improved: the council page says free, opens 7.30am daily, closing 6.30pm until 17 October 2026 down to 4.30pm from 22 November. Nothing else closed: no per-tree authority page, bfs_001's Ancient Tree Forum page gives only an Irish grid reference (J338696; pyproj missing, so not converted by hand). **Check:** a 2022 Ancient Tree Forum comment asks whether the Belvoir Oak still stands and gets no answer, so it deserves a life check; bfs_002 (Grass Tree) was not researched. 12 gaps recorded as 90-day dead ends, so bfs_002 is untested rather than empty.
- **Ronda:** all 6 trees got their Junta de Andalucía REDIAM ficha PDF (Arb_931, 875, 1011, 995, 871, 984, read by decompressing the PDF streams) as register record, with girth at 1.30 m (rnd_002's 510 cm was measured at 0.50 m and left out) and height. data/registers/andalucia-rediam.json already holds all six, so **the same REDIAM file is a cheap enrichment source for every other Andalusian city (Seville, Cordoba, Granada, Cadiz, Malaga, Jerez).** **Check:** rnd_004 (Castaño Santo) and rnd_006 stand on private land per the ficha (the former is "propuesto como Monumento Natural"); our access lines already say to keep to the public path or view from afar.
- **Bilbao:** dead end, nothing applied (no Basque file in data/registers, no per-tree ficha found; euskadi.eus returned nothing usable). **Check:** nothing confirms a bald cypress on the Deusto grounds (bil_002's only source is absolutbilbao.com), so its species and existence are unverified; the Misericordia park's night opening is disputed (one guide 24 hours, others fenced and shut); bil_001's "more than 170 years" comes only from a bilbao.eus news item. 12 gaps recorded as 90-day dead ends.
- **Lucca:** 13 trees matched at 0 to 31 m to MASAF sheets (E715/LU/09) with register id, girth and height (luc_003 height only; luc_005 is a group sheet with no figures; luc_012 folds twin sheets 32 and 33; luc_014 uses sheet 38 and also covers the yew on sheet 39). Orto Botanico hours and prices for five trees and the Torre Guinigi winter hours. **Check, hard rule 10:** the Lucca in Diretta article places the regional-list magnolia (luc_008, Via dell'Orto) in the cloister of Santa Maria Forisportam, which is the Pascoli primary school; if that is our tree it is school grounds and needs evidence of public access; luc_010's camphor may stand at Villa Giurlani (former Provveditorato offices), not a public street; luc_006's 700 cm Atlas cedar is a register figure and unusually thick. Regione Toscana per-tree schede were not searched.
- **Lausanne:** lsn_002 to 008 each got their own page on lausanne.ch (Service des parcs et domaines, "Patrimoine arboré") as register record, with girth and height from it (the pages do not say the girth is at 1.30 m) and, for lsn_002 to 007, the page's map marker as pin (25 to 130 m from ours). **Check:** lsn_008's marker (46.50922, 6.62252) is about 335 m from our pin (46.50685, 6.62522), past the 300 m limit, so it was not applied; **our lsn_008 pin may be wrong and deserves a look**. lsn_001 (Napoleon Oak) has no authority record and Wikipedia and the press disagree on its size (6.75 m / 35 m vs about 7 m / 30 m). Vaud cantonal data and Pro Arbore were not opened.
- **Hoorn (thin):** all 9 briefed trees match live LRMB entries (status 3, bezoekbaar and openbaar ja, agreeing with our access lines). Only hoo_013 changed: LRMB nr 1682399, a polygon covering 2 old elms (the register says only two remain as of 2026). The five register-coordinate pins for hoo_004, 005, 006, 008 and 009 were refused because the answer carried no source URL, **so those pins are unchanged although the register point sits on our pin; a pass that supplies the LRMB query URL as source would upgrade them to confirmed.** **Check:** hoo_004 and hoo_010 had strong fungal infection in 2024 (hoo_010 "op zijn retour"); hoo_004's register note says its twin maple was felled.
- **Graz:** all 16 trees (8 indexed) matched at 0 to 4 m to data/registers/graz-naturdenkmale.json (Stadt Graz open data, CC BY 4.0) and given the register id; grz_010 (Eggenberg copper beech) is absent from the city register, so it carries Steirisches Naturdenkmal no. 1506 from the de.wikipedia list, a weaker source. Schlosspark Eggenberg's price and hours (EUR 3 a day, annual pass EUR 20, 8:00 to 17:00 or 19:00 by season) confirmed on the Joanneum page and grz_009's access line rewritten. No measurements closed (Styrian Naturschutzbuch is behind a login). **Check:** the city register has an empty owner for grz_013 (Tegetthoffplatz hornbeam) and marks it not publishable, though the Wikipedia list puts it on the public square; worth a second look. The push credential expired at about 01:38 UTC, so commits from Alkmaar onward are local and rely on the Run health step.
- **Den Bosch:** seven trees got register records and measurements: dbo_005, 008, 010 from LRMB (nr 1697223, 1686317, 1696458) and dbo_004, 007, 009, 011 from the municipal layer (G-1058, G-0225, G-0229, G-0418; the LRMB entry for dbo_007 is a two-chestnut group 25 m away, and the only LRMB entry near dbo_009 is a weeping beech, so the municipal ids were used). **Retired dbo_006** (Copper Beech of Casinotuin): LRMB nr 1691687 is status 5, felled per an observation of 10-10-2015, so the municipal layer is stale; city meta, intro, FAQ, question copy and the silver maple's story (which cited it as a neighbour) now say eleven and drop it. The brief header said 12 trees but listed 8, none indexed.
- **Alkmaar:** alk_005, 011, 003, 007 and 008 matched live LRMB entries (status 3) and alk_007 (Jan Boon museum) got its "gratis entree" page as access source; alk_008's entry (351 cm, 25 m) covers a group of three elms. **Retired alk_004** (Elm of the Munnikenbolwerk, "thickest Dutch elm"): LRMB nr 1691013 is status 5, last observed 2015; moved to leads with a redirect, and the city meta, question meta, intro, FAQ and the two neighbouring plane and plume-elm passages now say eleven and no longer point at that elm. **Check:** alk_002 (Veenbos willow) matches nr 1697048, status 4 (Afgekeurd) with `zichtbaar: nee`, so it is not visible from public ground, a hard-rule-10 question about whether it should stay live. The FAQ said "All fourteen" before this edit, a stale count of an older version.
- **Gdansk (3 of 5 briefed):** gda_002 (Ash of the Royal Rifle Factory) got CRFOP PL.ZIPOP.1393.PP.2261011.7828 and 523 cm / 26 m / about 280 years from council resolution LV/1571/18 (read directly; CRFOP itself is behind Incapsula, 403 to fetchers); gda_003 (Oak of Wzgórze Luizy) the city list no. 551 and 630 cm / 20 m from the pl.wikipedia reproduction of the 1987 designation (probably larger now); gda_001 (burned Kupaly willow) the city monument no. 1095 and 495 cm. **Check:** gda_004 may be a copper beech (the city list has Fagus sylvatica 'Purpurea' at Czyżewskiego 26; three beeches stand at no. 31 and I could not say which is ours); the willow's 2000 girth was 460 cm per Wikipedia.
- **Rio de Janeiro (thin, 2 of 6):** heights from the Jardim Botânico's 2022 drone survey as reported by Agência Brasil (labelled preliminary): the Tom Jobim sumaúma 40.01 m, the giant African mahogany 49.1 m, plus the R$ 22 guided Giant Trees Trail price (2022). **Check:** jbrj.gov.br timed out (blocklist candidate), so current hours and prices are unconfirmed; rio_003 to 006 and the tombamento / imune-ao-corte decrees were never searched, **yet `--apply` recorded all 14 gaps as 90-day dead ends, so Rio is untested rather than empty.** Diário do Rio puts the Campo de Santana Emperor's Tree at about 150 years, unchecked against our record.
- **Oss:** the Lulboom of Megen (oss_007) got LRMB nr 1681077 (alive, status 3), 390 cm and 17 m. **FOR HIDDE (a decision, not git plumbing):** the register marks four of Oss's six trees status 5 (Dood/geveld): oss_002 nr 1680017 (felled 2019/20, a new lime planted), oss_003 nr 1692624 (felled 2023), oss_004 nr 1680015 (felled 2016, fungus) and oss_009 nr 1689509 (gone by 2002 storm). I retired them with `retire.py` and rewrote the copy, but that leaves a 2-tree place and `check_no_new_thin_places()` refuses it (Oss is not on data/thin-places-frozen.json, whose note says not to add to it). **I reverted the retirement**, so the four dead trees are still live on /oss. Either add `oss` to the frozen list and re-run `retire.py oss oss_002 oss_003 oss_004 oss_009` (the page copy then needs rewriting for two trees; `retire.py` prints what to change), or decide the page should go. This is the first time a shrinking place has hit the freeze; the same will happen to any city that loses trees to register deaths.
- **Helmond:** four trees (hlm_004, 013, 018, 020) matched to LRMB entries with register id, girth and height (three entries cover groups, so figures are presumably the thickest tree; noted in measure_source), plus October to November copper beech colour on hlm_020. **Retired hlm_014** (Nootka Cypress, Protestant cemetery): LRMB nr 1694365 is status 5, "in 2019 geveld, stobbe nog zichtbaar"; moved to leads with a redirect, and the city meta, question meta, context and FAQ now say nineteen (two cemetery trees remain). Only 5 of the 20 briefed trees had open register gaps. **Check:** the register notes a 2019 doubt about whether hlm_020's left tree is a copper beech.
- **Bratislava:** 6 trees got their Slovak protected-tree register number (499, 224, 227, 211, 214, 223) from data/registers/slovakia-chranene-stromy.json (no per-tree page). **Measurements deliberately not applied:** the authority sites (data.sopsr.sk 403, enviroportal.sk JavaScript-rendered) could not be read, and the only readable figures came from a citizen site (krizom-krazom.online: Grinava Poplar 705 cm / 40 m, Vajanského Sophora 311 / 18, Rudnayovo Ash 226 / 17, Škarniclova Paulownia 315 / 20, Šulekova Oak 360 / 25, Mišíkova Birch 94 / 8). They are recorded here for a later pass that can reach the register; `--apply` marked the measurement gaps as 90-day dead ends anyway. **Check:** brt_002's register row says jaseň štíhly (narrow-leaved ash, Fraxinus angustifolia) where we say European Ash.
- **Stuttgart:** four trees (stu_001, 004, 005, 006) got their LUBW Naturdenkmal steckbrief and number; the Hohenheim plane (stu_007) got 770 cm and about 34 m from the Nationalerbe-Bäume page (a foundation programme, not an authority register). **Check:** stu_007's existing LUBW source URL (id 2229013000033) is a Mannheim plane (82220000064), not the Hohenheim tree, **and should be removed from that record**; stu_006's register entry is a tree group at Solitude and does not name the ash; LUBW holds two "1 Winterlinde an der Bernhardskirche" entries (81110000075 used, 81110001703 unresolved); stu_002 is a Gedenkbaum, not a Naturdenkmal. The Hohenheim "free, year-round" flyer was only seen in a search summary, so I did not attach it.
- **Nagoya:** all 6 trees matched by name and place to their Environment Ministry giant-tree report (kyoju.biodic.go.jp, reports 39899, 79211, 40572, 40577, 40662, 40661; the site's keyword filter does nothing, so the pass paged the full listing). Nagoya Castle's price and hours (1,000 yen, 9:00 to 16:30; fees change 1 October 2026, no new figure published). Records' girth/height equal ours except ngy_004 (945 cm vs our 950), ngy_002's height (25 m vs our 21 m) and ngy_006 (a 2023 follow-up says 615 cm against the 1988/2000 540 cm); none applied. ngy_004's pin stays approximate. **Check:** ngy_004 is recorded as partly dead (一部枯損); ngy_006 stands at Yatomi Junior High School, so the school-grounds rule needs evidence the site is open.
- **York (no Woodland Trust data):** all 6 Museum Gardens trees got girth and height from the York Museum Gardens champion-trees page (which cites The Tree Register; the height of the girth measurement is not stated, assumed 1.3 m) and the gardens' free-entry hours (9am to 6pm). I dropped the pass's register claim: an owner's page naming The Tree Register is not a per-tree record, so no tree has a register field and none is rich yet. Pins stay open (no coordinate source; the gardens are too big for a location_site). **Check:** the Elaeagnus-leaved pear is the sole survivor of three and leans; the willow-leaved ash is declining at the top; the cut-leaved alder is decaying at the base.
- **Salamanca (thin, 1 of 4):** the University Sequoia (slm_001) is Junta de Castilla y León "Árboles notables" entry AS-SA-10 (Orden MAM/1156/2006), with 27.2 m and a girth of about 264 cm **derived from the register's 84 cm diameter times pi, assuming breast height, which the register does not state**. **Check:** La Gaceta (26 April 2026) says over 32 m, unresolved. slm_002 (Plaza de Anaya sequoia): the one news piece dates it to a 1976 transplant, worth checking against our age; slm_003 (Alamedilla cedar) is in the Junta's "Catálogo de Especies Singulares" (16 sheets in the city, not online) and its species is disputed (Lebanon, Himalayan or hybrid); slm_004 (Plaza de la Libertad yew) was confirmed alive in an article of 11 September 2026 but has no register or measurement. Ayuntamiento and university pages were not checked, so access lines stand; 10 gaps recorded as dead ends.
- **Hallstatt:** all 5 trees got their Land Oberösterreich Naturschutzbuch page (nd015, nd145, nd148, nd437, nd650) as register record; three got girth and four a height from the de.wikipedia list of the Bezirk Gmunden, which reproduces the designation text (figures date from designation, 1960 to 2006, and the measuring height is not stated, so treat as secondary). **Check:** hst_003 sits in "Untersee" per the Land page and "Obersee" per Wikipedia (same coordinates); hst_004's register page says about 200 years, hst_002's Wikipedia entry too. The 1989 PDF "Unsere Naturdenkmale" could not be text-extracted here and may hold hst_005's measurements. `lifecheck.py` was not run.
- **Ghent:** gnt_003, 004, 005, 006 and 008 got their Onroerend Erfgoed inventory page as register record; gnt_004's copper beech has 426 cm (the record does not say at what height). gnt_008's 449 cm was taken at 1.50 m and left out. gnt_007 (Massemen lime) still has no inventory record: Wetteren's page says protected by ministerial decree of 1 February 2001. No visiting hours were fetched, so access lines stand as they are, and 7 gaps went in as 90-day dead ends.
- **Salzburg:** 18 of 21 trees given their Land Salzburg Naturdenkmal id (NDM00019 to NDM00251) from the live OGD layer (CC BY 4.0, within 5 m of our pins; no per-tree page, so the layer URL is the source). No measurements anywhere: the layer has none and city pages give no girths. **Check:** szb_004's NDM00069 (from de.wikipedia) is not in the Land layer, so left out; szb_021 (Walserfeld pear) is not a Naturdenkmal; de.wikipedia puts the Fordhof plane and black pine in the park of the former Hotel de l'Europe while szb_009 says Fordhof housing courtyard (pins agree to 1 m, so a story framing to check), and NDM00114, the pine 43 m away, is folded into szb_009.
- **Bergamo (thin):** only the Torre Boldone horse chestnut (bgm_008) got MASAF sheet 01/L251/BG/03 with 391 cm and 27 m, and the Parco Marenzi bald cypresses an autumn-colour season. **Check:** this verify pass did not fetch the web (MASAF file only), so the Orto Botanico and Lombardia pages and any access lines were never looked at, yet `--apply` recorded the 7 remaining gaps as 90-day dead ends; **bergamo's access and register gaps deserve a real pass once that lapses or `enrich.py` is told otherwise.** bgm_004 and bgm_007 are MASAF "insieme omogeneo" groups with no per-tree figures. bgm_008 had a limb failure in 2021 and heavy pollarding in 2018.
- **Padua:** the three Parco Treves trees (pad_006 to 008) got MASAF sheet ids with girth and height from data/registers/italy-masaf.json; six Orto Botanico and Sophora trees got girths (and some heights) from RAMI (ilregistrodeglialberi.it, a citizen-run register, cited as measure_source only; I dropped its register-record claims, so those six carry no register field), and the five Orto trees got the garden's own hours and prices (EUR 15, 12 November to February, reduced 6; closed working Mondays). **Check:** pad_006 to 008's "Free, public park" is contradicted by two third-party listings that say Parco Treves has limited seasonal hours (the Comune's PDF could not be read); pad_010's cedar is rated "Pessima" in RAMI and may be declining; pad_011's 83 cm girth was dropped as implausible.
- **Freiburg:** all 7 trees given their LUBW Naturdenkmal steckbrief number (83110000009 to 83110000150; coordinates and designation dates match the de.wikipedia list on the four compared). Waldtraut (frb_001) got 67.1 m and 338 cm from the city Forstamt page (measured 2019); weeping beech October to November and lime June flowering set as best_time. frb_007's "at least 38 m" came from a newspaper and was left out; the Wikipedia 67.85 m for Waldtraut cites monumentaltrees.com and was not used. The Amtsblatt PDF is over 10 MB and curl aborted it. The Forstamt page gives walking routes to Waldtraut (2.5 km from tram stop Kyburg) if a writer wants them.
- **Aarhus:** all 7 trees linked to their Dansk Træregister (dendron.dk, Dansk Dendrologisk Forening, a society register rather than a government one) page, whose coordinates match our pins; Skåde's oak also gets the fredninger.dk page. Girth and height for aar_002, 004, 007, heights only for aar_001 and 003 (their girths were taken at 0.3 m and 1.0 m, so left out). **Check:** the Black Locust (aar_004) is "Levende, svag", with much of the crown dead; the Wild Service Tree's story must not repeat the 2010 single-stem claim (the 2022 record says two-stemmed, Denmark's thickest of the species); moesmus.dk fails to connect and lokalhistorieiaarhus.dk returned 455, blocklist candidates.
- **Toulouse:** all 10 trees matched by identical coordinates and species to the Toulouse Métropole `arbres-urbains` open dataset (Licence Ouverte v2.0) and given its record id (6372 to 129620) as register. No measurements (the dataset has no girth and an empty height class), so none of the ten reaches the richness bar yet. **Check:** tls_005 (Terre-Cabade) is flagged "France" rather than "Ville de Toulouse" in the register, probably the national A.R.B.R.E.S. label. 15 gaps recorded as 90-day dead ends.
- **Oxford (no Woodland Trust data used):** the Caucasian Elm (oxf_005) got the University Parks "Trees of the Parks" entry 15621, a 33 m height and access; the Bobart Yew an Ancient Yew Group record (a charity database, not a government register), a 13.7 m height (2014, before Storm Ciara) and Botanic Garden hours; the mulberry its hours; the Iffley Yew a 50 m churchyard location_site. I dropped the verify pass's register claim for the Pococke Plane (a college gardens homepage is not a record). **Check:** oxf_005's tie between entry 15621 and the North Walk tree is unproven (the Parks page returned Access denied); oxf_004's story says thickest Oriental Plane in Britain, but Trees and Shrubs Online names the Rycote Park plane, and its "viewable on the paid college visit" line is unconfirmed; an alumni article hints at a roped-off, possibly dead base on an Oxford elm, worth a look.
- **Nuremberg:** 12 trees matched by coordinate (0 to 16 m) to the Bavarian Naturdenkmal register file and given "ND Nr. N" plus the Nürnberg Naturdenkmalverordnung PDF as source (the city's per-tree sheets are not online). Measurements for two only: the Stadtpark lime (704 cm, 21 m, baumkunde.de 2021; nordbayern says "about 8 m", so the sources disagree) and the Bear Oak (680 cm, 28 m); June flowering on the lime. **Check:** nbg_017 has no register id because ND-05035 and ND-05036 sit 4 m and 17 m from it and the Platnersberg holds ten registered oaks; nbg_018's register-coordinate pin was refused (no source URL in the answer); nbg_016 access is unresolved; the Hallerwiese lime is described as lightning-damaged but resprouting.
- **Genoa:** 12 of 13 trees matched to MASAF sheets (register id, girth, height; gnv_007 and gnv_011 also link the Regione Liguria per-tree page), plus Villa Durazzo Pallavicini (EUR 11 weekdays, 13 weekends) and Villa Serra (EUR 3) prices and hours from the parks' own sites; gnv_009's old hours and price range were out of date and are replaced. **Check:** gnv_011's girth is now 608 cm (our 636 disagreed with both registers); gnv_007's height left out (MASAF 32 m vs Liguria 22 m). gnv_003's location_site was refused (the answer carried no source URL), so gnv_003 and gnv_004 are still not findable. Preflight notes five Genoa trees whose access says paid but `paid_entry` is not set.
- **Hawaii:** all 6 Kalopa trees got their Exceptional Trees register id (1159, 1160, 1184, 1185, 1202, 1203) and a girth converted from feet; heights only for the two ohia, because the register's koa (184 ft), hame (170 ft) and kopiko heights are implausible. Pins stay open: the register rounds to ~1.1 km. **Check:** the register does not say its circumference is at 1.30 m, and our "free, 7am-8pm" access line is not confirmed by the DLNR page.
- **Bordeaux:** all 10 trees matched by exact coordinates and species to the Bordeaux Métropole open dataset ec_arbre_p (Licence Ouverte): register id (gid), girth at 1.30 m and height (bdx_008's 3 m height dropped as implausible against 412 cm girth), plus Jardin Public and Parc Bordelais opening hours for 5 trees. No per-tree page exists, so no register URL. All 10 are VIVANT. **Check:** bdx_009 and bdx_010 stand in Mérignac, outside Bordeaux commune; the black poplar is a pollard (cepee); Chante-Grillon and Bourran access lines stay unconfirmed.
- **Miyazaki:** dead end, nothing applied. Four trees (myz_001 to 004) match Natural Monuments in data/registers/japan-bunkacho.json by name and distance, but that file has no per-tree id or URL and kunishitei.bunka.go.jp renders in JavaScript; **a session or run with a rendering fetch could add the heritage ids.** **Check:** myz_005 (Bald Cypress of Miyazaki Shrine) has no Natural Monument record; the only entry near it is a giant white wisteria 135 m away, so the page must not claim one. 14 gaps recorded as 90-day dead ends.
- **Menorca:** all 6 trees got their CAIB "Arbres singulars" ficha (a PDF, the authority's own sheet) as register record, with girth at 1.3 m and height from it. men_004's ficha covers a group of four planes; the largest values are used and the measure_source says so. **Check:** the ficha for men_006 (Pi Felip) records low vitality, a declining state and a branch lost in 2025, and men_001 records insect damage in 2003; the stories may want a condition line. No season set (no striking moment in those species files).

- **Zwolle:** LRMB ids (no per-tree page, so register_name plus nr) and girth/height for 7 trees, autumn or July season on the silver lime and two beeches. **Retired zwo_003, zwo_010, zwo_011, zwo_014:** the register entries at their coordinates are status 5 (Dood/geveld); moved to leads with redirects, city meta and FAQ now say fifteen. **Check:** zwo_016 is registered as Taxodium ascendens, our species line says distichum; zwo_001's register girth (757) disagrees with its own history text (719), so none applied. Access for the private-ground trees zwo_009 and zwo_013 stays open. Visits last 7 days: 1476.

## 2026-10-10 (night run, final window) - Enrichment: Heidelberg, Tilburg

- **Tilburg:** girth and height for the Zuiderpark five, the Korvelplein oak (plus a September fruit season), the twin planes (one register entry covers both trees) and the Kloosterpark oak (girth only, the register's two heights disagree). **Retired til_001, til_002, til_003** (Wilhelminapark elm, Frater Paullussehof lime, Wilhelminapark poplar): all three are LRMB status 5 (Dood/geveld). The city page now says seventeen, its hero is the weeping lime and the oldest tree is the Oisterwijk Marialinde; the Wilhelminapark page fell below the five-tree gate, so I deleted it and added a redirect to /tilburg in redirect-map.ts (not yet seen in a build). **Check:** the Zuiderpark trees are LRMB status 4 (Afgekeurd, not designated), so I took measurements only and no register fields; til_008 is registered as a Japanese wingnut where we say Caucasian.

- **Heidelberg:** all 6 Naturdenkmal trees got the LUBW per-tree steckbrief as register record, with the register's own number (82210001201 to 82210001227). The pages carry no girth, height or coordinate, so no measurements or pins; none of the six is finished enough to return to the index. 11 gaps recorded as 90-day dead ends. Removed the two stray _tmp scripts left by the Caserta pass. Shipped -3 trees earlier in the window came from retirements (Uneyama fir, Wageler beech), not a fault.

## 2026-10-10 (night run, late) - Enrichment: Caserta

- **Caserta:** all 20 trees got the Regione Campania scheda page as register record (the authority's own per-tree page) plus girth and height read from it. Pins left alone: several English Garden trees share one register coordinate, so it does not tell the trunks apart. 24 gaps recorded as dead ends. Preflight clean. A temp script scripts/_tmp_caserta.py could not be deleted (rm denied); remove it.

## 2026-10-10 (night run, 21:01 UTC) - Enrichment: Leeuwarden, Cambridge, Dubrovnik

- **Guimaraes:** all 8 trees: ICNF Arvoredo de Interesse Público ids, girth (metres converted to cm) and height from data/registers/portugal-icnf.json, matched to our pins by exact coordinates. gui_002's 19 m olive height dropped as implausible; gui_003's figures are the camellia group's; gui_005 carries an id but no measurement (a six-species group). Access lines were not re-checked (the Câmara page returned nothing usable).
- **Bologna:** 3 of 12: MASAF sheet 08/A944/BO/08 plus 500 cm / 24 m for the Villa Ghigi cedar (bol_009), MASAF sheet 15 for the grey walnut (bol_011), and the Orto Botanico's own access line for bol_007 and bol_011. **Correction:** bol_007's old "small entry fee" was wrong, the botanical garden page says "Ingresso gratuito". Worth a condition check: bol_011's butternut is described as under a safeguarding project. The regional Emilia-Romagna PDFs were not parsed.
- **Savannah:** dead end, nothing applied: no authority record, no live measurement source (Candler Oak's Wikipedia figures rest on an archived page the runner could not open), no OSM tree within 200 m of sav_003. 8 gaps recorded as 90-day dead ends.
- **Minneapolis:** 1 of 4: the Eloise Butler Hemlock (mnp_004) got the Minnesota DNR Native Big Tree Registry entry, girth 221 cm and height 22.6 m. MSP Magazine's coordinates put the hemlock grove ~280 m away and outside the gated garden; I did not apply that pin (a magazine is not a register) and our access line describes the garden, so **the pin and access line for mnp_004 need reconciling**. **Check:** MSP Magazine's "state champion" claim for the Windom Park basswood (mnp_003) is not supported by the DNR list, so any story carrying it needs a look.
- **Higashi-Hiroshima:** 17 of 18 briefed: Environment Ministry giant-tree database records (kyoju.biodic.go.jp, opened per tree, species matched) with 1988/2000 girth and height for hgh_001 to 003, 006 to 013, 015 to 020, and November ginkgo colour on hgh_018. hgh_019's 5 m height dropped as implausible. **Retired hgh_014 (Fir of Uneyama Shrine):** its only basis, report 53641, is the Ashuku Hachiman shrine fir (hgh_020); no record places a fir at Uneyama. Moved to leads with a redirect, counts now say nineteen. Pins unchanged (records carry no coordinates). Five records note partial dead wood (hgh_002, 008, 009, 011 and one more), as 1988/2000 condition notes only.
- **Enschede:** autumn colour on the weeping beech ens_005, a 9 m height for ens_014 (register records no girth). **Retired ens_003** (Beech of Landgoed Wageler): LRMB nr 1687596 at its coordinates is status 5 (Dood/geveld); moved to leads with a redirect, meta, question meta and the access FAQ now say thirteen/twelve and drop the beech. **Check:** the register calls ens_014 a beech but our record says hornbeam; ens_010 (Oriental Spruce) is only "potentieel monumentaal" in the register. The push credential expired mid-window, so the commits from Parma onwards are local and rely on the Run health step to push.
- **Cardiff:** thin, 1 of 4: the Blackfriars Champion Plane (crd_004) got the council's Bute Park tree record (Glamorgan Champion 2022, ref 828), girth 612 cm and height 24 m (April 2022), and a pin from the record's OS grid reference (ST 17804 76667), converted to WGS84 by hand because pyproj is missing (~40 m move). Girths for the chestnut and St Fagans oak came only from a Coed Cadw (Woodland Trust Wales) event report and were left out under the London gate. cardiff.gov.uk is behind a Cloudflare challenge.
- **Cordoba:** thin, 2 of 16: Palacio de Viana price and hours (patios EUR 8.50) for cdb_003, and the city's cemetery page hours for cdb_007 (the old closing times disagreed with the city's page). The municipal catalogue PDF (52 MB, image-heavy) cannot be read here (no PDF/OCR tooling), so register ids, measurements and pins for the catalogue trees stay open: **a session with OCR could unlock cdb_001 to 018**. Mezquita page does not mention the Orange Courtyard, so the "free, 08:30 to 19:00" line on cdb_001/002 is unconfirmed. alcazardelosreyescristianos.cordoba.es fails TLS.
- **Frankfurt:** 11 of 11 briefed trees: Hessian Naturdenkmal numbers (from the de.wikipedia list; five also link the city's per-tree page), measurements for the Eschersheimer Linde (archived city page), Sossenheim oak, Sachsenhausen oak, Höchst oak and Rödelheim ginkgo (via the Wikipedia list), Botanical Garden and Palmengarten hours and prices from their own pages (Palmengarten day ticket EUR 9, rising to 10 on 16 Oct 2026). frankfurt.de serves a Cloudflare challenge to fetchers (blocklist candidate; web.archive.org copies are the workaround). Pins unchanged (register coordinates equal ours).
- **Charleston:** thin, 2 of 3: Angel Oak height (19.8 m, City of Charleston page; its 25.5 ft circumference left out, point of measurement unstated) and a 50 m small-site pin for the Cistern Yard Oaks from the OSM way. No authority register entries found; middletonplace.org returns 403 and discovering.charleston.edu refuses connection (blocklist candidates).
- **Lyon:** thin, 3 of 13: Métropole de Lyon PLU-H "Arbre remarquable" layer ids for lyo_004, 011, 014 (no girth/height in that layer). The Tête d'Or trees and most others are not in the layer; the A.R.B.R.E.S. 2024 labels for lyo_001, 004, 005, 006 are confirmed by an article only (no per-tree label page). lyon.fr returned nothing, so no access hours.
- **Dordrecht:** 4 of 17: LRMB ids for dor_009, 015, 016; museum hours and EUR 17 price for dor_015/016 (the old "paid entry" line replaced by what the museum page states; it does not say whether the garden is open without a ticket); Huis van Gijn hours for dor_020; autumn colour on the copper beech dor_016. **Check:** dor_002 (Arend Maartenshof) access says "open almshouse courtyard" but the register has zichtbaar = nee; dor_005 (Sorghvliet) is a private estate with zichtbaar = nee; dor_019/020 ages disagree between the Huis van Gijn page (~1840) and the register (1860 to 1890).
- **Groningen:** 8 of 17: LRMB ids, girths and heights for gro_001, 011, 012, 014 to 017, 021; register pins on gro_014, 015, 016, 021 (within ~15 m of ours); autumn-colour best_time on the weeping beech gro_011. **gro_005 (Groenestein Plane):** the register's plane there is an Oriental plane in the villa's front garden, marked bezoekbaar nee, which does not match our "open park" access line. I rewrote the access line to say the park is public, the villa private, and the tree's side is unconfirmed; the tree is not retired, but a session should decide whether it stays (hard rule 10, front gardens). gro_002 to 004 (Sterrebos oaks, Noorderplantsoen ginkgo) have no LRMB entry nearby.
- **Ljubljana:** 2 of 4: the 1993 Ljubljana Center decree (Uradni list RS) as the register record for Furmanska lipa (natural monument), Botanical Garden hours for the Marmont linden. dedi.si is unreachable from the runner and ljubljanainfo.com sits behind Cloudflare (blocklist candidates), so the Streliška planes and all measurements stay open.
- **Cologne:** 5 of 5 got Naturdenkmal numbers (from the de.wikipedia reproduction of the city list; the city's own page 404'd), girths for kol_002 (530 cm) and kol_003 (375 cm), measured 2018-05-11 per the list. Pins for kol_004 and kol_005 stay open (the register has no coordinates).
- **Parma:** 5 of 5 got MASAF sheet ids (matched to our pins by coordinate and species); no web pages opened, so no fresh access or life check, and the regional Emilia-Romagna catalogue was not checked. No season set (plane and elm files have nothing above "nice").

**Rung:** none moved. No claims standing; the 3 unpushed commits (Dutch bulk LRMB, Stockholm, Zurich) are now pushed. 15 READY leads exist but recovery mode refuses new trees that are not rich (enriched()), so a write pass would fail preflight. enrich.py --next names Leeuwarden, whose open gaps are mostly season-only on trees that already carry register records; the last three enrichment passes (Stockholm, Zurich, Haarlem remainder) dead-ended, so no verify agent was dispatched. Window left unspent on purpose rather than burning tokens on a known dead end.
